### [1] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N2NMatcher，一个对函数内联具有鲁棒性的二进制程序分解与模块匹配框架，通过层级图神经网络预测锚点函数，实现锚点界定的分解与基于学习模块图嵌入的匹配，提升程序级二进制代码相似性分析的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序级二进制代码相似性分析（BCSA）中，现有方法通常按函数调用图（FCG）结构分解二进制程序并进行模块匹配，但函数内联会同时改变FCG结构和二进制函数语义，导致现有分解和匹配方法效果不佳。

Method: N2NMatcher首先通过实证研究考察编译设置下是否存在提供稳定模块边界的函数；随后利用层级图神经网络（编码opcode序列、控制流结构和FCG调用上下文构成的二进制ACFG-FCG表示）学习预测这些稳定函数作为锚点；最后执行锚点限定的分解，并使用学习的模块图嵌入进行模块匹配。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有工作相比，N2NMatcher在分解质量和模块匹配准确性上均有提升，从而实现了更有效的程序级BCSA。

Conclusion: N2NMatcher通过锚点函数预测和内联鲁棒的分解匹配策略，有效解决了函数内联对程序级二进制代码相似性分析的干扰问题，为软件抄袭检测、漏洞搜索和恶意软件分析提供了更可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [2] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种函数级度量方法，跟踪了Linux IIO子系统补丁历史中10,117个函数的轨迹，将补丁首版与最终版本进行逐版比较。研究发现代码评审高度保留语义（平均相似度0.990），但75.3%的函数从未被修改——这一高相似度主要由未编辑函数贡献，真正经过编辑的函数中，漂移集中在第一轮评审。控制相关变量后仍存在统计显著但微小的残余效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于代码评审的研究通常只研究最终合并的补丁，而不与首次提交的版本进行比较，导致对代码评审实际如何改变代码的认知存在偏差。作者希望从函数级别的粒度量化补丁从首版到最终落地版本之间的真实变化轨迹。

Method: 对Linux IIO子系统补丁历史中的每条函数在补丁序列的各编号修订版中进行追踪，构建10,117条函数轨迹。使用相似度评分将轨迹与无关函数对作为基线进行比较，区分"被编辑"与"从未被编辑"的轨迹，并分析编辑漂移在评审轮次间的分布。

Result: 表面上看轨迹几乎完全相似，但这是组成假象——75.3%的轨迹从未被文本修改，贡献了100%的相似度。仅考虑真正被编辑的轨迹，语义仍大部分保留（平均相似度0.990 vs 基线0.909），漂移集中在第一轮评审，主要因为后几轮包含更多未被修改的函数而非编辑变得更保守。控制后仍有统计显著但微小的残余效应。

Conclusion: 近天花板相似度可能反映功能意图被保留，也可能反映当前度量工具无法检测小而局部编辑的重要性。作者将此工作定位为初步探索，并规划了后续研究方向。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [3] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过固定进化方案，在八种编程语言和三种基础模型上系统分析自进化智能体（self-evolving harnesses）的演化产物，揭示其编码的是可解释的"补偿层"——由语言工程需求和模型行为缺口共同塑造，而非不可解释的基准适配脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于自进化智能体的研究仅报告聚合性能提升，未深入分析演化产物究竟编码了什么——是基准特定适配、语言特定工程知识，还是对底层模型局限的补偿。这种不透明性阻碍了对该类系统的理解和可迁移性判断。

Method: 作者在Multi-SWE-Bench的八种编程语言和三种基础模型上固定进化方案，记录每个编辑的失败信号并将其作为可证伪的契约，使每个修改在进化后可归因。通过对比最小种子和手工设计的mini-SWE-agent脚手架，分析缺陷类型、语言生态细节与性能增益间的对应关系，并通过蒸馏实验验证共享核心的可迁移性。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 进化循环在大多数单元中超越最小种子和手工脚手架，但存在两个无效区域；(2) 增益补偿可恢复的执行缺陷，缺陷质量近零时增益也近零，主导缺陷类型因单元而异；(3) 演化产物在跨语言间共享抽象剧本，但语言生态系统机制几乎互不相交；(4) 共享核心可蒸馏为单一通用harness，而生态边际抵抗蒸馏，需要原生再进化。

Conclusion: 演化harness应被理解为一个可读的补偿层，其内容由语言的工程需求和模型的行为缺口共同决定，而非不可解释的基准调优脚手架。共享核心支持通用化蒸馏，但生态差异需要本地再进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [4] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过组织内四个AI项目的探索性案例研究，识别出六个信号指标（即"偏见气味"），用于在开发过程中识别可能引发公平债务的潜在来源，将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平债务源于AI开发缺陷可能导致的社会危害，尽管学界日益关注AI公平性，但实践中开发者如何识别公平债务的潜在来源仍缺乏实证证据。

Method: 对同一组织内的四个AI项目进行探索性案例研究，通过对25名专业人员开展半结构化访谈和开放式问卷收集数据，辅以内部沟通渠道和项目文档的观察，并通过迭代定性编码、备忘录和持续比较进行分析。

Result: 识别出六个反复出现的指标，命名为"偏见气味"：上下文过度简化、数据集不平衡、指标不充分、临时性测试、个体多样性认知不足、团队组成同质化。这些气味涵盖技术与人因两个层面，预示着可能引入或强化偏见并导致公平债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏见气味将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域，通过可观察的指标为将公平性纳入软件质量保证提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [5] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了以太坊智能合约安全分析工具Mythril（基于符号执行）产生大量误报的问题，通过分析误报的根本原因，设计了一套基于规则的方法来减少误报，实验证明在不影响真实漏洞检测能力的前提下显著降低了误报率，提升了工具的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约的安全性至关重要，但基于符号执行的漏洞检测工具（如Mythril）会产生大量误报，这些误报会消耗开发人员大量时间和精力去排查，从而分散了对真实漏洞的修复资源。因此，有必要分析误报的根源并改进工具算法，以提高其可靠性。

Method: 首先分析Mythril生成的漏洞报告中误报的普遍性及其根本原因；然后基于分析所得洞见，设计一套规则化的方法；针对Mythril中影响最大的漏洞类型实现这些规则，并评估其降低误报的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的规则化方法能够在不牺牲真实漏洞检测能力的前提下，显著减少误报数量，从而增强了Mythril工具的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过分析误报的根因并设计针对性的规则，可以有效提升基于符号执行的智能合约漏洞检测工具的准确性，使其更值得信赖，同时不损害其对真实漏洞的检测能力。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [6] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia是一个Eclipse插件，通过将结构化依赖图可视化与基于LLM的代码解释集成在同一交互式图上，帮助Java程序理解。该工具基于四个支柱：多边类型依赖图、基于图感知Callee剪枝（GACP）的可审计LLM解释、克隆检测覆盖层和CVE风险覆盖层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码理解工具要么只提供静态结构视图，要么只提供LLM生成的孤立文本解释，缺乏两者的深度整合。论文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，通过共享交互图将结构化依赖关系与LLM智能解释统一起来，使开发者既能看见代码结构，又能获得可追溯、可验证的语义解释。

Method: Comprendia包含四个核心模块：(1) 支持实时搜索和多布局的多边类型依赖图；(2) 采用图感知Callee剪枝（GACP）策略的LLM解释引擎，利用图距离、继承折叠和边类型权重来筛选相关被调用方法，生成跨LLM家族可复现且可追溯到图中可见节点的提示；(3) 克隆检测覆盖层，突出重复代码并建议提取到父类的重构机会；(4) 基于OSV.dev的CVE风险覆盖层。

Result: 论文在一个包含已知克隆和漏洞的Java项目上演示了Comprendia，展示了统一的图基础设施如何在保持开发者控制权的同时支持代码理解。该方法通过GACP策略确保了LLM解释的可审计性、可复现性和可追溯性。

Conclusion: Comprendia通过将结构依赖可视化和LLM驱动的代码解释集成到单一交互界面，为Java程序理解提供了一种可控、可审计的增强方案。其成功示范表明，将图结构作为LLM提示的指导框架，能够有效提高代码解释的准确性和可信度，同时通过克隆检测和CVE扫描增强了工具的实际工程价值。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [7] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过前瞻性预注册随机实验，检验大语言模型在将理论翻译为可执行程序时，不同呈现格式（结构化干预契约 vs 连贯散文）是否系统性改变生成的程序行为。320个单次生成程序在两个LLM快照和五种理论下进行测试，结果表明两种主要假设均未获支持，相同理论的可识别性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523），格式对程序行为的影响未达到预先设定的分类学几何预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口头理论无法直接运行，将其转化为可执行模型需要对变量、干预和交互作出选择。此前缺乏系统性证据表明LLM在执行这种翻译时，理论的外部呈现格式（而非理论内容本身）是否会系统性改变生成程序的行为特征。作者旨在量化格式效应，并测试是否能根据行为几何学对理论来源进行可分类的识别。

Method: 采用前瞻性预注册随机实验设计：16个独立随机分配位将32对渲染器槽位分为结构化干预契约和连贯散文两种格式；两个固定的LLM快照翻译5个匿名理论账户，生成320个预授权的单次生成程序（冻结的稀疏二次语言）；采用确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；两个主要终点评估跨模型匹配距离缩减和闭集同一账户可识别性；使用精确随机化推断和27个预注册支持标准，覆盖符号线性与幅度排序两条流水线。

Result: H1和H2两个主要假设均返回注册裁定"不支持"（NOT_SUPPORTED）；108个标准评估中仅19个通过。同一账户可识别性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523，注册阈值为0.80）。仅有一个H2匹配距离终点在符号线性流水线中显著并通过多重性校正，但对应的幅度排序结果未达到注册效应量下限，因此不满足联合支持规则。总体而言渲染格式未产生预先预测的、均匀的、族不变的、可分类的行为几何学。

Conclusion: 该研究在LLM理论到程序翻译中，确定了规范格式效应的具体边界：呈现格式并未系统性改变程序行为到可预测、可分类的程度。研究提供了完全可审计的随机化设计、数据集和软件，为研究可执行形式化提供了方法论基础设施，有助于未来对LLM可执行翻译行为的进一步实证探索。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [8] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出从开发者与LLM代理的交互轨迹中提取可复用的开发者偏好技能框架，通过规则引导的自举和基于证据的细化生成个性化技能，并利用可复现的重放框架和LLM模拟器进行评估。在206个真实会话上的实验表明，个性化技能相比无技能基线仅带来小幅且不稳定的提升，而跨开发者聚合的通用技能反而取得最大且最稳定的收益。研究揭示了开发者个性化在何种条件下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理从代码补全工具演变为复杂软件工程任务的求解器后，开发者在长期交互中会反复表现出个人偏好。现有技能学习工作主要针对任务特定技能，缺乏对开发者特定技能的泛化能力研究，因此需要探究从交互历史中提取的开发者偏好技能能否泛化到未来任务，以及其在何种条件下比通用技能更有效。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 通过规则引导的自举（rule-based bootstrapping）和证据支撑的细化（evidence-grounded refinement）从交互轨迹中生成个性化技能；(2) 使用可复现的重放框架并搭配交互式、轨迹条件化的LLM人类开发者模拟器来评估技能有效性。实验基于13位开发者的206个真实开发者-代理会话，对比个性化技能与无技能、通用技能、其他开发者技能三种基线。

Result: 实验发现：个性化技能相对无技能基线仅带来小幅且不一致的提升；跨开发者池化的通用技能获得最大且最稳定的收益；当开发者偏好频繁出现且历史中包含与未来任务相关的多个示例时，个性化技能才更有效。

Conclusion: 广泛可转移的程序性知识比开发者特定的偏好信号更稳健。研究为开发者特定个性化在何时有效提供了实证洞察，表明开发者个性化并非总是最优选择，通用技能在多数情况下更为可靠。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [9] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoX Genesis框架，将软件工程中的持久性从"持久化代理"转向"持久化项目"，通过递归世界模型让有限生命周期的代理协作完成超长时间尺度的软件开发任务（如编译器构建、模块重写），并在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统的开发周期远超任何单个编码代理的寿命。现有代理系统通过持久化会话、记忆、管理器或共享上下文来维持连续性，但这依赖"持久化代理"的范式。作者认为应转向"持久化项目"的范式——项目本身保持持久，允许本地代理保持有限生命周期。

Method: Genesis将软件表示为持久递归世界：每个本地世界由接受版本和仓库路径定位；有限生命周期的代理提出局部变更；递归委派将工作跨路径转移；仅被接受的后果推进持久版本历史。这一组织结构消除了对持久代理的依赖，使开发通过递归分解和版本推进得以长期持续。

Result: (1) 使用DeepSeek V4 Flash构建了约25万行Rust C编译器（运行超120小时、归档1000+代理片段、仅花费44美元），通过完整c-testsuite及大部分LLVM和Csmith测试；(2) 用GLM 5.2生成的编译器世界中，多次代理替换后继续开发仍保持完整测试性能；(3) 将13个MESA模块（超10万行Fortran）重新实现为近9万行Rust workspace，在6个数值工作负载上实现1.55-6.87倍中位加速。

Conclusion: 长期地平线的软件开发可以围绕持久化项目而非持久化代理来组织。Genesis证明了有限生命周期代理通过递归世界模型和版本历史推进，同样能完成大规模、跨代理世代的复杂软件工程任务，且在成本和性能上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [10] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CausalRepair是一个基于最小因果上下文的对话式自动程序修复框架，采用上下文感知静态切片和执行轨迹动态切片的双重切片策略，构建紧凑的因果相关上下文来引导迭代修复，在Defects4J数据集上修复313个bug，超过现有方法并将平均修复成本降至每个bug 0.029美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动程序修复方法存在因果性缺口：测试上下文可能包含噪声或不完整，而基于静态分析得到的源代码上下文常包含无关和未执行的代码，这会误导LLM无法识别真正的故障根因。

Method: CausalRepair采用双重切片策略：(1) 上下文感知的静态切片用于净化测试语义；(2) 基于执行轨迹的动态切片用于捕获源代码中精确的运行时依赖。二者结合构建紧凑、因果相关的上下文，通过对话方式引导LLM进行迭代修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0和Defects4J-Trans上使用DeepSeek-V3进行评测，CausalRepair正确修复了313个bug，超越了ReinFix和TSAPR等最先进方法，并将平均修复成本降低至每个bug 0.029美元。

Conclusion: 通过构建最小因果上下文而非全量或嘈杂的上下文，CausalRepair有效地解决了LLM-based APR中的因果性缺口问题，在修复效果和修复成本两方面均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3. CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [11] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Cursor中配置型提示文件（.cursorrules）进行了实证研究，收集分析了11,427个GitHub仓库中的12,110个.cursorrules文件，并通过对65个文件的定性分析开发了65类代码编码本。研究发现此类文件自2024年中期快速出现，主要在小型、低活跃度、单人维护的仓库中采用；内容以代码质量和工程实践为主，安全相关内容较少；且.cursorrules与.mdc文件之间存在主题连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示工程研究主要聚焦于会话式提示，而配置型提示文件（如.cursorrules）作为AI辅助编程中重要的静态提示机制尚未被充分研究。作者希望通过实证研究填补这一空白，理解开发者如何通过配置文件表达编程意图。

Method: 收集并分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个.cursorrules文件，进行定量分析以刻画其分布、演化和维护特征；同时对65个提示文件进行随机抽样定性分析，开发了一个包含65类编码的编码本，覆盖编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全性考虑等方面。

Result: 研究发现：.cursorrules文件自2024年中期快速涌现；其采用集中在小型、低活跃度、单人维护的仓库中，暗示多为玩具项目而非专业开发；文件内容以代码质量与工程实践指导、项目结构与配置、可维护性为主，安全相关内容出现频率较低；此外，.cursorrules与现行标准的.mdc文件在主题和话题上具有连续性。

Conclusion: 配置型提示文件在AI辅助编程中扮演重要角色但当前实践仍不够成熟。内容集中于代码质量和工程实践而忽视安全性，且多用于非专业场景。研究揭示了配置文件从.cursorrules向.mdc演化的连续性，为未来提示工程研究和工具设计提供了基础性认识。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [12] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的多LLM集成框架，用于汽车硬件在环（HiL）验证中的传感器级故障分类。该框架利用故障注入录制的紧凑证据表示，结合异构大语言模型（LLM）的输出，通过置信度加权投票提升诊断鲁棒性、排序质量、置信度可靠性和可解释性。在汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统上，Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B）实现了0.917 Top-1准确率和0.902 MCC，优于任何单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL验证中产生大量多变量测试记录，但分析仍依赖人工审查，基于规则的方法受限，且需可解释的诊断决策。现有机器学习和深度学习故障诊断方法需要大规模标注数据、跨工况泛化能力差，且对预测的解释能力有限，亟需一种无需大量标注且可解释的自动故障诊断方案。

Method: 提出可解释的多LLM集成框架：(1) 将故障注入录制构建为紧凑的证据表示作为输入；(2) 使用多个异构LLM（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B等）进行独立故障分类；(3) 通过置信度加权投票策略集成不同模型的输出，形成最终诊断决策。评估涵盖汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统、三种驾驶场景和十种单故障类别。

Result: 单一模型中Mistral Small 24B表现最佳（Top-1准确率0.903，MCC 0.887，Brier分数0.102）。Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B + Qwen2.5 32B + Phi-4 14B）采用置信度加权投票，将场景平均结果提升至Top-1准确率0.917、宏F1 0.913、MCC 0.902，并具有最佳校准性能。Top-5集成未优于Top-3配置，表明模型互补性比集成规模更重要。

Conclusion: 协调的多LLM推理能够为汽车HiL验证提供鲁棒、校准良好且工程师可解释的故障分类方案。模型之间的互补性比简单地增加模型数量更关键，置信度加权的Top-3集成是实现性能与可解释性平衡的最优配置。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [13] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PPProbe，一个针对概率Datalog分析的特化冲突提取器，用于发现输出报警中的最小不可满足子集（MUSes），解决边际概率不保证联合可满足性的问题。PPProbe利用Datalog推导图结构引导搜索并剪枝，在70个基准上比现有MUS枚举器吞吐量高2.5-24倍，平均过滤47.7%的互不一致报警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog扩展（如指针分析、数据竞争检测、侧信道分析）允许按似然度排序报警，但引入了确定性分析中不存在的新挑战：最终输出的报警可能各自看似合理却相互矛盾，因为边际概率不保证联合可满足性，导致开发者调查在现实世界中不可能同时出现的报警组合，浪费大量精力。

Method: PPProbe将报警间的不一致形式化为最小不可满足子集（MUSes），利用Datalog推导图的结构特性而非通用的MUS枚举方法来引导搜索优先寻找可疑冲突，并通过自底向上的UNSAT推理剪枝搜索空间。

Result: 在70个基准上（覆盖功率侧信道分析、数据竞争检测、语义差异分析、贝叶斯网络推理），PPProbe比最先进的MUS枚举器吞吐量高2.5-24倍，识别的冲突能保守估计减少误报，平均过滤47.7%的互不一致报警。

Conclusion: PPProbe验证了针对概率Datalog分析定制冲突提取器的有效性，通过对Datalog推导图的深度利用实现了显著的性能提升和报警过滤效果，为概率静态分析工具的实用性提供了重要改进。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [14] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文延续了之前为NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予基于三重图语法（TGGs）的正式语义的工作，并引入了一种"跳过语义"（skip semantics）来弥补TGG在翻译重叠查询方面的不足，从而实现模型与视图之间的自动化、增量式同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视图的开发是管理复杂系统复杂性的重要技术，但视图定义语言需要在易用性与精确形式语义之间取得平衡。为了支持视图间工作的受控集成与自动化同步，需要为其提供正式语义。

Method: 延续先前将NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予基于三重图语法（TGGs）的形式语义的工作，识别出在翻译重叠查询方面存在的缺口，并提出为TGG规则引入"跳过语义"（skip semantics），允许规则根据应用上下文跳过某些规定动作。

Result: 引入了TGG规则的跳过语义，解决了重叠查询翻译支持不足的问题，为实现模型与视图间自动化、增量式同步铺平了道路，同时进一步验证了TGG的表达力和可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅推进了NeoJoin语言的形式化进程，还作为TGG表达力的案例研究，证明了通过跳过语义扩展TGG可以有效支持视图定义语言中的重叠查询翻译。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs. We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [15] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了量子软件模式挖掘工具，添加了两个匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，在Qrisp框架上达到0.712的微F1分数（相比无扩展的0.449），并通过构建组合图来研究模式实现如何组装、为何共现，同时发布了开源挖掘流水线qpa及相应的知识库和模式使用数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式提供了构建量子程序的高级抽象，但缺乏关于这些模式在实践中如何被采用的实证证据。本研究旨在通过工具化和大规模量化分析，填补量子模式在实际代码中采用情况的研究空白。

Method: 在已有的61模式目录和知识库基础上，扩展了挖掘工具（新增两个匹配通道和一个词汇扩展步骤），在80个项目上应用，并在Qrisp框架上进行定量准确率评估。同时构建记录框架组件间调用关系的组合图并存入图数据库，用于分析模式的组装方式、共现原因及检测来源。

Result: 在Qrisp框架上达到0.712的微F1分数，对比无词汇扩展时的0.449；全部23个模式在实际代码中都有出现。发布了开源挖掘流水线qpa，包含映射286个框架组件（来自五个来源）的知识库，以及模式使用数据集。

Conclusion: 通过工具化方法验证了量子模式在实际项目中的普遍存在性，词汇扩展步骤显著提升了检测准确率。组合图分析揭示了模式实现如何在框架内部组装和共现的内在机制。开源发布支持量子软件模式采用与演化的可复现研究。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [16] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍GitSkills数据集，包含从282,200个公共GitHub仓库中收集的3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，用于研究agent技能的采用、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能（skill）是一种新的软件开发工件格式，自2025年10月Anthropic推出后迅速在GitHub上大量扩散（数百万文件），但它们以自然语言为主、由模型在运行时概率性地选择、无编译器验证，且没有中央注册表，主要靠复制文件夹传播。SE研究社区缺乏记录这一新兴工件群体的数据集，因此需要构建一个来支持实证研究。

Method: 作者从GitHub上收集了3,797,117个SKILL.md文件（来自282,200个公共仓库），保留每个文件出现的仓库、路径和内容哈希。将相同内容文件分组为1,877,981个不同内容，并为每组的一个代表文件补充全文、解析的前置元数据（front matter）、文件夹内容、仓库元数据，以及一部分文件的提交历史。所有数据存储在一个自包含的SQLite文件中。

Result: 构建了包含近380万个SKILL.md文件、约190万个不同内容的数据集，以单一SQLite文件形式发布，支持研究agent技能的采用、复用、结构、作者、维护和安全性等方向。

Conclusion: GitSkills填补了agent技能这一新兴软件工件实证研究的数据空白，为研究社区提供了可扩展的数据基础设施。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>

*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地记录了编码代理（coding agents）的内部架构，提出了一个开源最小化编码代理Ark及其轻量级基准ArkBench，用于教学、研究和实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理已成为AI辅助软件开发的主要接口，但其内部架构鲜为人知，缺乏像编译器或操作系统那样系统化的架构描述。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者记录了编码代理的主要架构组件及其交互和执行流程，并构建了开源的Ark（Agent Research Kit）——一个保持现代编码代理核心机制但强调简洁性的最小化代理。同时引入了ArkBench基准，包含10项代表性软件维护与演化任务，并利用gpt-5.4-mini模型进行验证。此外，使用新提出的架构分类法对Ark与最先进的编码代理进行架构对比。

Result: 使用gpt-5.4-mini，Ark成功解决了10项任务中的8项，且token消耗适中。文中还通过与最新架构分类法的对比，展示了Ark与现有编码代理在架构层面的异同。

Conclusion: Ark和ArkBench为编码代理的教学、研究与实验提供了一个实用的基础平台，填补了该领域缺乏系统性架构文档的空白。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [18] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Configuration Management (ACM)，一个框架无关的配置治理参考模型，用于异构智能体系统。ACM通过类型化配置项、不可变修订、配置-运行时分离、依赖感知影响传播等语义，将异构原生配置规范化投影为规范配置图，并基于此实现统一的治理语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMOps和AgentOps平台仅支持编排与可观测性，缺乏统一的配置治理模型来将异构智能体系统表示为连贯的、版本化的配置，导致跨框架和运行时环境的一致性、可审计性和可复现性难以保障。

Method: ACM框架包含：1) 类型化且独立版本化的Agentic配置项(ACIs)；2) 不可变修订与基线；3) 显式配置-运行时分离；4) 生命周期与保障语义；5) 依赖感知的影响传播；6) 运行时溯源。通过语义投影将异构原生配置归一化为规范配置图，在图上运行统一治理语义。提供支持LangGraph、CrewAI和OpenAI Agents SDK的Python参考实现。

Result: 27个治理场景与9个定量影响传播案例的评估显示，三个框架在投影后产生治理等价的ACM表示和可复现的治理结果。影响语义被形式化为有限格上的单调传播，建立了收敛性、终止性和最小不动点唯一性。

Conclusion: 共同治理语义可以支持异构智能体执行抽象之间的可复现性、可审计性、依赖分析和互操作性。影响传播的数学形式化（有限格上的单调传播）保证了治理分析的收敛与确定性。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>
