### [19] [Do LLM Recommenders Know When They're Hallucinating? Auditing Confidence Calibration in Catalog Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10008)
*Srijith Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对四个零样本LLM推荐系统（Mistral Large、Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Sonnet 4.6）在三个目录（MovieLens-25M、Amazon Toys、Yelp）上的幻觉率与置信度校准进行联合审计。发现幻觉率因目录而异（0-8.3%），但所有模型均系统性欠自信（verbalized confidence 67-86 vs 实际准确率92-100%），与常见的过度自信认知相反，归因于elicitation mismatch；基于置信度的保形拒绝阈值最多只能减少0.7pp幻觉率，但代价是4-21pp覆盖率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM推荐系统的审计仅将目录外输出视为二值越界率，未考察模型是否知道自己正在幻觉（即置信度校准问题）。本文旨在弥补这一空白，联合评估幻觉率与口头化置信度校准。

Method: 对四个独立厂商的零样本LLM推荐器在三个目录上进行幻觉率（OOD@10）与置信度校准（ECE、Brier、可靠性图）的联合审计，按物品流行度分层，并测试基于口头化置信度的保形拒绝阈值以减少幻觉。

Result: 幻觉率因目录而异（MovieLens 0-0.2%、Amazon 4.5-8.3%、Yelp 2.2-8.4%），但即使幻觉为零时置信度也显著失准（MovieLens上ECE高达0.223）。所有四个模型在全部12个单元（3目录×4模型）中系统性欠自信，这与通常强调的LLM幻觉过度自信相反。保形拒绝阈值最多减少0.7pp幻觉率，但需付出4-21pp的覆盖率代价，表明欠自信通道无法区分正确项与幻觉项。

Conclusion: LLM推荐系统的审计应同时报告校准与OOD指标；口头化置信度的欠自信本质是elicitation mismatch（"Just Ask"引出了通用推荐质量评分而非目录成员概率）；应使用目录锚定的elicitation而非通用置信度提示。

Abstract: LLM recommenders for top-$K$ item suggestion regularly emit titles outside the target catalog. Prior audits measure this as a binary out-of-domain rate; none ask whether the model knew it was hallucinating. We jointly audit hallucination rate (OOD@10) and verbalized-confidence calibration (ECE, Brier, reliability) for four zero-shot LLM recommenders from four independent vendors (Mistral Large, Llama-3.3-70B, GPT-OSS-120B, Claude Sonnet 4.6), not grounded or fine-tuned systems, across three catalogs (MovieLens-25M, Amazon Reviews 2023 Toys, Yelp Open Dataset), stratified by item popularity. Hallucination is catalog-dependent (0--0.2\% on MovieLens, 4.5--8.3\% on Amazon, 2.2--8.4\% on Yelp), but verbalized confidence is materially miscalibrated even when hallucination is zero (ECE up to 0.223 on MovieLens despite 0\% OOD). All four LLMs are systematically \emph{under}-confident across all twelve cells, verbalizing a mean of 67--86 on items they recommend with 92--100\% accuracy. This is the opposite of the over-confidence usually emphasized in LLM-hallucination work. The under-confidence is best read as an \emph{elicitation mismatch}: ``Just Ask'' elicits a generic recommendation-quality rating, not a catalog-membership probability. A conformal abstention threshold over verbalized confidence reduces hallucination by at most 0.7\,pp across $α\in \{.05, .10, .15, .20\}$, at 4--21\,pp of coverage cost: the under-confident channel cannot separate correct items from hallucinations, so the threshold mostly removes correct items. We recommend that audits of LLM recommenders report calibration alongside OOD, and use catalog-anchored elicitation rather than generic confidence prompts.

</details>


### [20] [ConnectionMind: Leveraging Social Networks and Large Language Models for Personalized Recommendation at Meta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10187)
*Haoyu Han,Yuming Liu,Lei Huang,Lizhu Zhang,Jiliang Tang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ConnectionMind是Meta提出的生产级推荐框架，将社交网络结构（用户、好友、群组、创作者页面）与大规模语言模型（LLM）紧密集成，将推荐形式化为图推理问题，通过LLM策略在异构图上发现从用户到候选物品的个性化路径。采用两阶段训练策略（先SFT初始化推理策略，再端到端RL微调），已在Meta大规模推荐管线中部署，A/B测试实现视频观看时长提升0.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐模型往往忽视社交关系信号或将其独立对待，缺乏将多关系上下文（朋友关系、群组归属、创作者互动）与海量异构内容（文本、视频）进行整合推理的能力，难以实现细粒度的个性化推荐。

Method: 构建连接用户、物品、好友、群组和创作者页面的异构知识图谱，将推荐问题转化为图推理任务（发现用户到候选物品的个性化路径）；使用基于LLM的策略在这些图结构上进行推理并指导推荐决策；采用两阶段训练：第一阶段在大型用户-物品交互轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT）初始化推理策略，第二阶段通过端到端强化学习（RL）优化模型在社交图中推理的能力。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明ConnectionMind优于代表性基线方法；在Meta大规模推荐管线中部署并通过在线A/B测试验证，视频观看时长提升0.43%，展示了可衡量的实际生产影响。

Conclusion: ConnectionMind证明了将社交网络图结构与LLM推理能力深度融合在超大规模生产推荐系统中的可行性和有效性，为社交平台推荐系统提供了可扩展、可解释且具备推理能力的新范式。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems on social media platforms such as Meta must model complex social relationships, including friendships, group memberships, and creator interactions, alongside massive and heterogeneous content such as text and video. Traditional recommendation models, however, often omit these signals or treat them independently, lacking the reasoning capability to integrate multi-relational context for fine-grained personalization. We present ConnectionMind, a production-ready recommendation framework that tightly integrates the social network structure with large language models (LLMs) to enable scalable, interpretable, and reasoning-aware personalization in Meta. ConnectionMind constructs a heterogeneous graph connecting users, items, friends, groups, and creator pages, and formulates recommendation as a graph reasoning problem: discovering personalized paths from users to candidate items. An LLM-based policy is employed to reason over these graph structures and guide recommendation decisions. To train the system at scale, ConnectionMind adopts a two-stage learning strategy. We first perform supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on large-scale user-item interaction trajectories to initialize the reasoning policy, followed by end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) to refine the model's ability to reason over social graphs for personalized recommendation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ConnectionMind compared to representative baselines. More importantly, ConnectionMind has been deployed in Meta's large-scale recommendation pipeline and has been evaluated through online A/B tests, achieving a 0.43% improvement in video watch time. These results demonstrate measurable real-world impact in a production recommendation system.

</details>


### [21] [Sequential Modality Dropout for Robust Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10240)
*Guanqun Yang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequential Modality Dropout (SMD)，一种仅需四行代码、与架构无关的训练策略，通过在整个用户交互历史上以概率p独立擦除每个模态流，使多模态序列推荐模型在部署时面对模态缺失仍能保持较高的推荐准确率。实验证明SMD在四个骨干模型和四个Amazon数据集上将文本保留率提升1.0-3.2倍，且在极端95%逐项缺失率下保留了61%的HR@10（相比无SMD的22%提升2.8倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐模型假设每个物品都携带所有模态（图像和文本），但真实产品目录中经常缺少图像或文本。当某个模态在服务时不可用时，在完整数据上训练的模型会损失大量推荐准确率。需要一种方法使模型对模态缺失具有鲁棒性。

Method: SMD在训练期间，对每个模态流（图像和文本）以概率p在整个用户交互历史上独立进行擦除（dropout），迫使模型学会不依赖任何单一模态进行下一项预测。此外，提供了一个可选的跨模态重建损失以进一步增强严重模态缺失下的鲁棒性。该方法是一个仅需四行代码的、与架构无关的训练变更。

Result: 在四个骨干模型（MM-SASRec、IISAN、MISSRec和fMRLRec）和四个Amazon领域上的实验表明，SMD将文本保留率提升1.0到3.2倍，且几乎不损失全模态准确率；在95%逐项缺失的极端条件下，保留61%的HR@10，而无SMD仅保留22%（2.8倍改进）。可选的跨模态重建损失在严重文本缺失下将保留率从90%提升至98%。

Conclusion: SMD是一种简单有效、架构无关的训练策略，使多模态序列推荐器对部署中实际遇到的模态缺失具有鲁棒性，以极低的成本显著提升模型在模态不完整场景下的推荐性能。

Abstract: Multi-modal sequential recommenders assume every item carries every modality, but real product catalogs often miss images or text, and a model trained on complete data loses much of its recommendation accuracy when a modality is unavailable at serving time. We propose Sequential Modality Dropout (SMD): during training, each modality stream (image and text) is independently erased with probability p for an entire user interaction history, so the model learns to predict the next item without relying on any single modality. We measure robustness by retention, the fraction of a model's full-modality accuracy (HR@10) that survives when a modality is removed at test time. Across four backbones (MM-SASRec, IISAN, MISSRec, and fMRLRec) on four Amazon domains, SMD raises text retention by 1.0 to 3.2x at essentially no cost to full-modality accuracy; under an extreme 95% per-item missing rate, it retains 61% of HR@10 versus 22% without (a 2.8x improvement). An optional cross-modal reconstruction loss further lifts retention from 90% to 98% on a simple additive backbone under severe text missingness. SMD is a four-line, architecture-agnostic change that makes multi-modal sequential recommenders robust to the missing modalities they actually encounter in deployment.

</details>


### [22] [DualSpectralCF: Training-Free Sign-Aware Spectral Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10247)
*Guanqun Yang,Tong Qi,Xiaoxue Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DualSpectralCF，一个无训练的谱协同过滤框架，通过将显式负反馈（如差评、讨厌点击等）编码为带符号的输入信号和带符号的item-item算子，无缝嵌入到任意谱协同过滤骨干（如ChebyCF、GF-CF、Turbo-CF）中，仅增加两个标量超参数，即可在5个数据集上始终匹配或超越无符号基线，Recall@20最高提升+32.6%，并在冷启动用户上尤其受益（最高+29.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实推荐平台收集了大量显式负反馈（1星评分、讨厌点击、不信任、低观看比例等），现有的学习型符号感知推荐器（sign-aware recommenders）虽然能利用这些信号提升准确率，但依赖梯度训练、成本高昂；而另一类无训练的谱协同过滤方法虽然计算效率极高，却只能处理正向交互。两种范式各有优势却互不相通，本文旨在将符号感知能力引入无训练的谱协同过滤框架，兼得其利。

Method: DualSpectralCF包含两个核心组件，可附加到任意形式为 r̂_u = F(M)r_u 的谱骨干上：(1) 带符号的输入信号 r_u^±，将用户显式的不喜欢项编码为负信号；(2) 带符号的item-item算子 M^±，混合"喜欢者相似"与"不喜欢者相似"两类相似性。整个框架与骨干无关，仅需两个标量超参数(γ, κ)。作者在ChebyCF、GF-CF和Turbo-CF三个谱骨干上实例化该框架。

Result: 在5个符号感知基准数据集上评估：所有DualSpectralCF实例在所有5个数据集上均匹配或超越其无符号骨干版本，Recall@20最高提升+32.6%（配合骨干特定(γ,κ)调参）；在默认固定超参数(γ=-0.5, κ=0.1)下，DualSpectralCF-Cheby的Recall@20提升为+1.9%至+16.0%。相比SIGformer，该方法速度快7.7至155.3倍，同时能达到其70.7%至90.7%的准确率。对冷启动用户（Epinions上仅1至5个训练项）Recall@20最高提升+29.2%。

Conclusion: DualSpectralCF成功弥合了符号感知学习型推荐器与无训练谱协同过滤之间的鸿沟，以极低的额外成本（仅两个标量超参数、无需训练）将显式负反馈纳入谱过滤流程，在准确性、效率与冷启动场景上均取得了显著收益，为现实推荐系统中负反馈信号的利用提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world recommendation platforms routinely collect explicit negative feedback such as 1-star reviews, hate-button clicks, distrust between users, and very-low watch-ratio videos. Learned sign-aware recommenders exploit this signal for clear accuracy gains, but only at the cost of gradient-based training. In parallel, a line of training-free spectral collaborative filtering methods matches or beats learned graph recommenders at a fraction of the cost, yet operates on positive interactions alone. We bridge these two lines with DualSpectralCF, a training-free framework of two components that attach to any spectral backbone of the form $\hat{\mathbf{r}}_u = F(\mathbf{M}) \mathbf{r}_u$: a signed input signal $\mathbf{r}_u^{\pm}$ that encodes the user's explicit dislikes, and a signed item-item operator $\mathbf{M}^{\pm}$ that blends like-together and dislike-together similarity. The framework is backbone-agnostic and adds just two scalar hyperparameters. We instantiate DualSpectralCF on ChebyCF, GF-CF, and Turbo-CF, and evaluate on five sign-aware benchmarks: every instance matches or beats its unsigned backbone on all 5 datasets, with Recall@20 lifts up to +32.6% with backbone-specific $(γ, κ)$ tuning and +1.9% to +16.0% for DualSpectralCF-Cheby at the fixed default $(γ= -0.5, κ= 0.1)$, and the family runs 7.7 to 155.3$\times$ faster than SIGformer while reaching 70.7% to 90.7% of its accuracy. Sign-awareness helps most for cold-start users, with up to +29.2% Recall@20 on Epinions users with 1 to 5 training items.

</details>


### [23] [GenRec: An LLM-Backed Recommendation Ranker at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10257)
*Ying Li,Shradha Sehgal,Arjun Rao,Rein Houthooft,Yaochen Zhu,Ashish Rastogi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Netflix的GenRec系统——一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的推荐排序器，采用两阶段框架：第一阶段将开源LLM适配到Netflix数据，第二阶段用推荐排序特定数据和奖励信号进行后训练。论文聚焦第二阶段，详细描述了从传统判别式排序器（数千个工程化特征）向LLM驱动的排序器（基于语言化的用户历史和上下文）的转变，并通过大规模A/B测试验证了该方法在离线与在线指标上的显著性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖大量手工工程化特征和定制的模型架构，难以充分利用自然语言中丰富的用户、内容和上下文信息。Netflix希望探索LLM能否通过自然语言直接建模用户行为，从而改变推荐系统的构建范式。

Method: 采用两阶段训练框架。Phase 1将开源LLM适配到Netflix数据，理解目录和会员行为；Phase 2使用推荐排序特定数据进行后训练，涵盖输入语言化与上下文工程、后训练数据构建、奖励整合、模型架构设计，以及基于prefill-only推理方法的成本受限服务设计。

Result: 大规模A/B测试结果显示，GenRec模型使用显著更少的Phase-2标注训练样本和输入信号，即可在离线与在线指标上取得相对于当前生产排序器的统计显著性提升。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的推荐系统可能将推荐范式从特征工程转向上下文工程，从特定架构转向共享基础骨干。同时论文提出了在真实世界资源约束下服务此类系统的实践经验和启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping recommender systems by enabling richer modeling of users, content, and context directly in natural language. At Netflix, we are exploring this direction through GenRec, an LLM-backed recommendation ranker built on top of an in-house foundational LLM. GenRec follows a two-phase framework: Phase 1 adapts an open-source LLM to Netflix data, developing deep understanding of the catalog and member behavior while balancing capabilities such as content understanding and instruction following. Phase 2 post-trains this foundation model with recommendation-ranking specific data, labels, and reward signals, aiming to align the ranker with business requirements and long-term member satisfaction.
  This paper focuses on Phase 2 and the transition from a traditional discriminative ranker with thousands of engineered features to an LLM-backed ranker driven by verbalized user histories and context. We describe our design for input verbalization and context engineering, post-training data construction, reward integration, model architecture, and a cost-constrained serving design based on a prefill-only inference approach. We report results from a large-scale A/B test comparing GenRec against the current production ranker model, where we show that a GenRec model trained with substantially fewer Phase-2 labeled training examples and input signals can achieve statistically significant gains in offline and online metrics. We discuss how LLM-backed recommenders could shift the recommendation paradigm: from feature engineering to context engineering, and from bespoke architectures to shared foundation backbones. We also outline practical lessons for serving such systems under real-world resource constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering: Rethinking Graph Collaborative Filtering as Tree Collaborative Filtering with Curvature-Aware Propagation Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10297)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering (NTCF)，从树结构视角重新审视图协同过滤，基于局部度不平衡分数（离散Ricci曲率代理）为每个节点分配个性化的传播深度，以解决均匀传播深度导致的外围节点过度平滑和枢纽节点探索不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图协同过滤对所有节点应用统一的传播深度，忽略了真实交互图中节点局部连接性的巨大差异。外围节点因过深的传播而遭受过度平滑（over-smoothing），而枢纽节点的词结构又未被充分挖掘。这种"一刀切"的传播策略无法适应不同节点的局部拓扑特性。

Method: NTCF将每个节点的局部邻域重新解释为有根树结构，并提出一个闭式（closed-form）的局部度不平衡分数作为离散Ricci曲率代理。基于该曲率分数，为每个节点分配个性化的传播深度：正曲率的（外围）节点使用较浅的传播层以防止过度平滑，负曲率的（枢纽）节点使用较深的传播层以充分探索其邻居。论文还给出了理论分析：(i) NTCF严格推广了NGCF，当所有曲率诱导的深度调整消失时退化为NGCF；(ii) 曲率感知的传播调度在深层保留比均匀深度传播更多具有判别性的信息。

Result: NTCF在三个公开数据集上的广泛实验验证了其优越性，能够超越大多数广泛使用的GCF骨干模型，并且可以作为骨干网络集成到现有的高级自监督模型中，替换原始骨干网络后进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: NTCF通过引入节点级自适应传播深度机制，有效地解决了图协同过滤中因均匀传播导致的节点表征退化问题，不仅从理论上证明了其与NGCF的泛化关系和信息保留优势，还在实验上验证了其作为通用骨干的灵活性和有效性。

Abstract: Graph Collaborative Filtering (GCF) has become the dominant paradigm in modern recommender systems by modeling user-item interactions as a bipartite graph and propagating embeddings through a fixed number of message-passing layers. However, applying a uniform propagation depth to every node ignores a fundamental property of real interaction graphs: nodes differ substantially in their local connectivity, so peripheral nodes quickly suffer from over-smoothing while hub-like nodes remain under-explored beyond their immediate neighborhood. In this paper, we revisit GCF from a tree-structured perspective and propose Neural Tree Collaborative Filtering (NTCF), a framework that re-interprets each node's local neighborhood as a rooted tree and assigns a node-specific propagation depth based on a closed-form local-degree-imbalance score that serves as a discrete Ricci-curvature proxy. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that (i) NTCF strictly generalizes NGCF, degenerating to NGCF when all curvature-induced depth adjustments vanish (a lower bound on its representation power), and (ii) the curvature-aware schedule retains strictly more discriminative information at deep layers on positively-curved (peripheral) nodes than uniform-depth propagation. NTCF can achieve higher performance than most widely used GCF backbone models and can be integrated into existing advanced self-supervised models as a backbone, replacing their original backbone to achieve enhanced performance. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of NTCF.

</details>


### [25] [Persona Conditioning as an Assessor-Sensitivity Probe for LLM-Based IR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10385)
*Samaneh Mohtadi,Pietro Bernardelle,Joel Mackenzie,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型作为信息检索相关性评估者时，角色人称（persona）条件化如何影响判断可靠性和系统比较结果。通过五种任务导向的评估者角色和六种LLM骨干模型，在TREC DL20和RAG24数据集上发现评估者敏感性是结构化而非均匀的，且系统级排名一致性在高容量模型中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作IR评估中的相关性评估者，但评估者"框架"（如角色设定）如何影响判断可靠性和下游系统比较尚不清楚。作者希望用人称条件化作为一种诊断机制，暴露LLM评估者的敏感性。

Method: 从PersonaHub和NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA两个互补来源构建五种评估者角色（意图理解、领域专家、对比判断、证据验证、全局搜索质量评估），与标准UMBRELA基线对比。在六个LLM骨干模型上对TREC DL20和RAG24数据集进行评估，采用基线对比、系统级排名一致性分析和局部排名位移分析。

Result: 评估者敏感性是结构化而非均匀的：判断通常接近基线，但会改变评估严格度、证据阈值或解释侧重而非产生广泛的相关性反转。高容量模型保持系统排名一致性，小模型放大角色诱导的不稳定性。敏感性集中在特定检索系统类型上（DL20的神经排序/重排序系统、RAG24的RAG管道）。角色来源（persona source）影响小于角色类型和模型容量。

Conclusion: 人称条件化判断可作为一种受控的敏感性探针，用于压力测试基于LLM的IR评估管道，并识别其评估结果对评估者框架敏感的系统。这为构建更稳健的LLM评估方法论提供依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as relevance assessors in information retrieval (IR) evaluation, raising questions about how assessor framing affects judgment reliability and downstream system comparison. We study persona conditioning as a diagnostic mechanism for exposing LLM assessor sensitivity. Using task-oriented personas drawn from two complementary sources (PersonaHub and NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-USA), we instantiate five assessor roles emphasizing intent interpretation, domain expertise, contrastive judgment, evidence verification, and global search-quality assessment, compared with a standard UMBRELA baseline. Across six LLM backbones on TREC DL20 and RAG24, our analyses reveal structured rather than uniform assessor sensitivity. Judgments usually remain close to the baseline while shifting assessment strictness, evidential threshold, or interpretation emphasis rather than producing widespread relevance reversals. At the system level, high-capacity models preserve system-ranking agreement, while smaller models amplify persona-induced instability. Local rank-displacement analysis shows sensitivity concentrates on particular retrieval systems and system types, especially neural ranking/reranking systems on DL20 and RAG-oriented pipelines on RAG24. Persona source matters less than assessor role and model capacity. These findings position persona-conditioned judging as a controlled sensitivity probe for stress-testing LLM-based IR evaluation pipelines and identifying systems whose evaluation outcomes are sensitive to assessor framing.

</details>


### [26] [Post-Calibration Reliability Reranking of Relevance Decisions via Label-wise Monotone Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10406)
*Inwoo Tae,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出标签级单调可靠性投影（MRP）方法，通过学习标签级单调函数将校准后的置信度映射为正确性可靠性分数，在不改变原始预测标签和类别概率的前提下，根据残余风险对固定预测进行重排序。在六个信息获取相关性数据集和多种后验校准器上，MRP提升了可靠性重排序性能和平均回退效用，同时保持了全覆盖率准确率和ECE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的后验校准方法仅将置信度与平均正确率对齐，无法消除同一校准置信度水平下依赖预测标签的可靠性差异。这会导致系统过度信任错误预测，或不必要地推迟正确预测。作者希望弥补这一差距，即在保持校准的同时，进一步捕获标签级别的残余风险信息。

Method: MRP学习一组标签级单调函数，将每个类别的校准置信度映射为正确性可靠性分数。该方法保持原始预测标签和类别概率不变，仅通过学习得到的可靠性分数对固定预测进行重排序，从而根据残余风险提升排序质量。作者还分析了将MRP可靠性分数嵌入到顶类概率几何空间的可能性，作为兼容性分析。

Result: 在六个信息获取相关性数据集和多种后验校准器上，MRP在可靠性重排序和平均回退效用方面均取得了改进，同时保持了全覆盖率下的准确率和期望校准误差（ECE）。消融实验表明主要增益来自标签级残余可靠性而非全局置信度重映射。

Conclusion: MRP有效解决了校准后仍存在的标签级可靠性差异问题，为检索系统提供了一种在不损害原有校准性能的前提下进行可靠性重排序的新方法。该方法的实现将公开发布。

Abstract: Web search, product search, and question-answering retrieval systems often assign a relevance label and confidence score to each query-candidate pair. The relevance label describes how well a page, product, or passage matches the query, while the confidence often guides downstream use or fallback decisions. Post-hoc calibration is therefore needed because misaligned confidence can make systems over-trust wrong predictions or unnecessarily defer correct ones. However, calibration mainly aligns confidence with average correctness, and does not remove predicted-label-dependent reliability differences that remain within the same calibrated confidence level. We address this gap with Label-wise Monotone Reliability Projection (MRP), which learns label-wise monotone functions that map calibrated confidence to correctness reliability while preserving the original predicted labels and class probabilities. The resulting reliability score reranks fixed predictions according to residual risk. Across six information access relevance datasets and multiple post-hoc calibrators, MRP improves reliability reranking and average fallback utility while preserving full-coverage accuracy and ECE. Structural ablations show that the main gains come from label-wise residual reliability rather than from global confidence remapping. We further analyze when MRP reliability scores can be embedded back into top-label probability geometry, showing that this projection is useful as a compatibility analysis but is distinct from the main reliability-reranking objective. The implementation will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [27] [Towards Efficient Reasoning in LLM-Based Recommender Systems via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10447)
*Linh Dieu Le,Tong Chen,Shazia Sadiq,Hongzhi Yin,Ming Jin,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REAM，首个面向推荐系统推理压缩的模型合并框架。不同于传统均匀合并方法，REAM在注意力头级别进行细粒度合并，为每个注意力头分配不同的合并系数，选择性将快思考模型的简洁行为注入慢思考模型，在不牺牲推荐准确率的前提下将推理长度最多缩短24.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的推荐系统越来越多地采用慢思考模型（逐步推理后预测），准确率更高但推理轨迹冗长、推理成本高。现有训练式推理压缩方法适应成本高，推理时压缩方法脆弱且难以扩展。这些局限促使模型合并成为一个有前景的无训练方向，通过合并慢思考模型与快思考模型来平衡推荐准确率与推理简洁性。

Method: 提出REAM框架，区别于传统方法对模型组件施加统一合并系数，REAM在单个注意力头级别进行细粒度合并。每个注意力头根据其对关键推理证据的贡献度和对参数变化的敏感度被分配一个独特的合并系数，从而选择性地将快思考模型的简洁行为注入慢思考模型，实现推理压缩而不损害推荐质量。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，REAM将推理长度最多缩短24.3%，同时在保持推荐准确率方面优于竞争性的模型合并基线方法。

Conclusion: REAM验证了注意力头级别细粒度模型合并作为推荐系统推理压缩的有效手段，为在共享参数空间中转移特定行为提供了新的无训练方案，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model-based recommender systems are increasingly adopting slow-thinking models that generate step-by-step reasoning before making predictions, often achieving higher accuracy than fast-thinking models that predict directly. However, their reasoning traces are often unnecessarily verbose, increasing inference costs without commensurate accuracy gains. Existing training-based approaches to reasoning compression often incur substantial adaptation costs, while inference-time methods are brittle and difficult to scale. These limitations motivate model merging as a promising training-free direction for transferring specialised behaviours between models in a shared parameter space. In particular, merging a slow-thinking model with a fast-thinking counterpart provides a natural mechanism for balancing recommendation accuracy and reasoning conciseness. To this end, we propose, to our knowledge, the first model merging framework for reasoning compression in recommender systems. Unlike conventional merging methods that apply uniform merge coefficients across model components, our method performs fine-grained merging at the level of individual attention heads, capturing heterogeneous patterns in recommendation reasoning. Each attention head is assigned a distinct merge coefficient according to its contribution to critical reasoning evidence and its sensitivity to parameter change, enabling selective injection of the concise behaviour of the fast-thinking model into the slow-thinking model and reducing reasoning verbosity without compromising recommendation quality. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that our method reduces reasoning length by up to 24.3% while outperforming competitive model merging baselines in maintaining recommendation accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linhledieu/REAM.

</details>


### [28] [When Do Anchor-Based Pointwise LLM Rerankers Help? Retriever Quality, Statistical Scope, and Anchor Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10528)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对基于锚点的逐点LLM重排序方法（以GCCP/PAGC为代表）进行了以复现为先导的受控分析。初始复现仅达到0.24 nDCG@10（原文为0.66），识别并恢复8个未记录的实现细节后，复现结果与原文差距在1.6%以内。研究发现对比评分核心思想在统计校正下依然稳健，但两个固定设计选择并不可靠：对比分数与标准逐点相关性分数结合仅在BM25检索器下有益，使用更强的稠密检索器（如E5）时几乎无增益；原文复杂的锚点构建方法不必要，简单的句子交错锚点在多个数据集上匹配或超越原方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点式逐点LLM重排序方法声称能以逐点成本恢复跨文档上下文，但其有效性条件尚不明确。作者希望系统性检验该方法在何种条件下真正有效，特别是识别哪些设计选择贡献了实际收益。

Method: 采用复现优先（reproduction-first）的研究范式：首先仅依据论文文本进行独立重实现，发现与报告结果严重不符后，逐一识别8个未记录的实现细节并恢复至1.6%误差内；随后基于验证过的实现进行受控组件级压力测试，分别检验对比评分、锚点构建策略、分数组合方式及不同检索器和LLM骨干下的表现。

Result: 对比评分核心思想在严格统计校正下稳健；但对比分数与标准逐点相关性分数结合仅在BM25作为第一阶段检索器时有帮助，对E5等强稠密模型几乎无增益；原文复杂锚点构建方法不必要，简单的句子交错锚点即可匹配或超越其性能。这些发现在不同LLM骨干上（包括4-bit量化的72B模型）一致。

Conclusion: 锚点式逐点重排序确实有效，但其收益主要来自对比评分而非复杂的聚合和锚点构建选择；且其优势出现条件比原文评估所显示的范围更窄。

Abstract: Anchor-based pointwise LLM reranking scores each candidate against a shared reference passage to recover cross-document context at pointwise cost. We study when this actually helps, using GCCP/PAGC as a representative method. Our study is reproduction-first. We use reproduction as a starting point for a controlled component-level stress test of anchor-based pointwise reranking. Our initial reimplementation, based only on the paper text, achieves 0.24 nDCG@10 instead of the reported 0.66, revealing that several undocumented implementation details are necessary to reproduce the method. After identifying and recovering eight such details, we reproduce the reported results within 1.6% and use the validated implementation for controlled analysis.
  We find that the core contrastive scoring idea is robust under rigorous statistical correction. However, two design choices held fixed in the original paper are less reliable. First, we find that combining the contrastive score with the standard pointwise relevance score helps when the first-stage retriever is BM25, but gives little or no benefit when the first-stage retriever is a stronger dense model such as E5. Second, the paper's more complex method for constructing the anchor is unnecessary. A much simpler anchor, built by interleaving the top-ranked sentences, matches or outperforms it across datasets. These findings are consistent across different LLM backbones, including a 4-bit quantized 72B model. Overall, anchor-based pointwise reranking is effective, but its gains come mainly from contrastive scoring rather than from the more complex aggregation and anchor-construction choices, and they appear under narrower conditions than the original evaluation suggests.

</details>


### [29] [Multi Interests for Joint Search-Recommendation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10535)
*Xiangchen Pan,Wei Wei,Huakang Niu,Zhicong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MIJSR多兴趣混合序列建模框架，从结构和语义两个视角对搜索-推荐混合行为序列进行多兴趣挖掘与自适应融合，通过跨域行为融合、多兴趣挖掘和多任务预测三大模块提升搜索与推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域统一建模框架虽然能弥补搜索与推荐行为域间差异，但忽略了混合序列中不同场景下兴趣表达的差异，缺乏对用户多兴趣的细粒度挖掘。

Method: MIJSR包含三个模块：(1) 跨域行为融合——通过对比学习对齐query和item的表示；(2) 多兴趣挖掘——结构上通过子序列划分和掩码设置提取搜索兴趣、推荐兴趣和跨域兴趣，语义上利用query语义信息进行聚类并对混合序列进行语义分割以构建语义多兴趣；(3) 多任务预测——将多个兴趣自适应融合并结合侧信息，使用渐进式分层提取模型进行多任务预测。

Result: 在两个开源数据集上的大量实验表明，MIJSR通过细粒度地提取用户多兴趣，能够进一步提升搜索和推荐的准确性。

Conclusion: 从结构和语义双重角度对搜索推荐混合序列进行多兴趣建模是有效的，细粒度的多兴趣提取能够更好地理解用户在不同场景下的偏好，从而提升搜索与推荐的联合性能。

Abstract: Search and recommendation are crucial for understanding user preferences. More and more studies are attempting to jointly model search behavior and recommendation behavior, by integrating user active search and passive recommendation behavior data to better mine user preferences. However, although existing cross-domain unified modeling frameworks can effectively compensate for the differences in behavior between domains, they overlook the expression of interests in different scenarios under mixed sequences. In this study, we propose a multi-interest-based mixed sequential modeling framework MIJSR, which performs multi-interest mining and adaptive integration on search recommendation mixed sequences from both structural and semantic perspectives. Specifically, our model can be roughly divided into three modules: cross-domain behavior fusion, multi-interest mining, and multi-task prediction. Firstly, we align the representations of query and item through contrastive learning training. Then, we extract the multi interests of the mixed behavior sequence from both structural and semantic perspectives. Structurally, we extract search interests, recommendation interests, and cross interests through subsequence partitioning and mask settings; In terms of semantics, we use the semantic information of queries for clustering and perform semantic segmentation on mixed sequences to construct semantic multi interests. Finally, the adaptive fusion of multiple interests is combined with other side information to use a progressive layered extraction model for multi-task prediction. Extensive experiments on two open-source datasets have shown that our model can further enhance its accuracy in search and recommendation by extracting users' multi interests at a fine-grained level. Codes are available at https://github.com/pxcstart/MIJSR.

</details>


### [30] [DistilVDR: A Compact End-to-End Visual Document Retriever via Dual-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10636)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Ziyi Wang,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DistilVDR是一个524M参数的端到端视觉文档检索系统，通过从单个8B视觉语言教师模型中双向蒸馏而来，使用逐点余弦对齐损失，无需相关性标签或负采样。它在ViDoRe基准上达到86.9%的教师性能，索引体积比最强sub-1B多向量基线小15.6倍，索引速度快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索(VDR)系统依赖数十亿参数的大模型，索引整个语料库速度慢且服务成本高。先前的压缩方案要么从头训练较小的多向量编码器，要么仅对查询侧进行蒸馏，均无法端到端获得紧凑的单向量检索器。

Method: DistilVDR采用双向蒸馏，从一个冻结的8B视觉语言教师模型的嵌入空间中提取监督信号，使用逐点余弦对齐损失训练524M参数的编码器学生模型。学生模型采用非对称的编码器-only架构：文档侧集中视觉能力，查询侧仅保留70M参数。发布了两种共享编码器和训练、仅在文档编码器视觉补丁数量上不同的变体：DistilVDR-HiRes和DistilVDR-Fast。

Result: DistilVDR-HiRes在ViDoRe v1+v2+v3上达到61.74的平均NDCG@5（相当于8B教师的86.9%），在分辨率敏感的v3基准上领先所有复现的sub-1B基线；DistilVDR-Fast达到59.98且视觉token预算减少3倍。两种变体存储100万文档的索引比最强的sub-1B多向量基线小15.6倍，索引语料库速度快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过双侧蒸馏可以在不牺牲太多性能的前提下，将VDR系统压缩为紧凑的单向量编码器，显著降低索引成本和部署开销，同时保持对高分辨率场景的竞争力。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval (VDR) is dominated by multi-billion-parameter models that are slow to index at full corpus scale and expensive to serve. Prior compression routes either train a smaller multi-vector encoder from scratch or distil only the query side; neither yields a compact single-vector retriever end-to-end. We present DistilVDR, a 524M end-to-end VDR system distilled bilaterally from a single 8B vision-language teacher under a pointwise cosine alignment loss. All supervision comes from the frozen teacher's embedding space, which was itself trained with relevance supervision, so the student objective needs no relevance labels, negative sampling, or contrastive term. We match VDR's text-query and image-document input asymmetry with an asymmetric encoder-only student that concentrates visual capacity on the document side and keeps the query side at 70M parameters. We release two variants that share the same encoders and training and differ only in the document encoder's visual-tile budget: DistilVDR-HiRes attains 61.74 average NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 (86.9% of the 8B teacher) and leads every reproduced sub-1B baseline on the high-resolution-sensitive v3 benchmark, while DistilVDR-Fast attains 59.98 at a 3 times smaller visual-token budget. Both variants store one million documents in a 15.6 times smaller index than the strongest sub-1B multi-vector baseline and index the corpus an order of magnitude faster. The code is available at https://github.com/Ryenhails/NanoVDR.

</details>


### [31] [ENTLORE: A Graph-Grounded Benchmark for Latent Organizational Reasoning in Enterprise Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10679)
*Akrin Zheng,Alexander Wu,Alaia Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ENTLORE，一个图锚定的企业问答基准构建框架，从常规文档、组织表和运营记录中重建可审计的企业世界，涵盖2341篇文档和907个问题（显式查找、跨源组合、潜在组织推理三类），并在56种模型和访问配置下评估，发现潜在组织推理问题即使提供黄金文档仍有30.4%无法回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业问答基准虽然提供了多源证据，但往往预设了答案路径，只能测试对已陈述事实的组合能力，而无法评估从语料中缺失的目标关系中恢复隐含组织关系的能力（即潜在组织推理）。企业日常记录本质上是工作副产品，所需组织关系在异构来源中是隐式的，因此需要新的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: ENTLORE框架从常规文档、权威组织表和运营记录中重建可审计的企业世界。使用版本化组织约定在真值图中验证派生关系，生成完整黄金答案和证明证书。发布的匿名化版本仅暴露文档语料，隐藏私有结构和目标关系。评估56种模型和访问配置，涵盖显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理三类问题。

Result: 提供的释放世界以诱导实体图或可导航知识库结构化时，可获得最强的可部署结果。但即使提供黄金文档，潜在组织推理问题仍有30.4%无法回答，而显式和组合问题的未回答率分别为12.6%和6.2%。这表明企业QA不仅依赖文档召回，还取决于隐含组织关系是否变得可用。

Conclusion: 企业问答不仅依赖于文档召回能力，还取决于隐含组织关系能否被有效利用。ENTLORE基准揭示了潜在组织推理是企业QA中的核心挑战，即使有完美的文档检索，这些隐含关系仍难以被模型自动恢复。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering is framed as retrieving internal documents and generating grounded answers. Routine enterprise records, however, are work by-products in which required organizational relations remain implicit across heterogeneous sources. Existing benchmarks provide realistic multi-source evidence, but often materialize a predefined answer path and therefore test the composition of stated facts rather than recovery of a target relation absent from the corpus. We call the latter capability latent organizational reasoning.
  We introduce ENTLORE, a graph-grounded benchmark construction framework that reconstructs an audited enterprise world from routine documents, authoritative organizational tables, and operational records. Versioned organizational conventions certify derived relations in a truth graph, enabling complete golden answers and proof certificates. The aligned anonymized release exposes only the document corpus while withholding private structure and target relations. ENTLORE contains 2,341 documents from three source types and 907 questions spanning explicit lookup, cross-source composition, and latent organizational reasoning, evaluated across 56 model and access configurations. Structuring the released world as an induced entity graph or navigable knowledge base gives the strongest deployable results. Yet supplying gold documents still leaves 30.4% of latent questions unanswered, versus 12.6% and 6.2% for explicit and compositional questions. Enterprise QA therefore depends not only on document recall, but also on whether implicit organizational relations become usable. The benchmark, data, and code are publicly available at ENTLORE.

</details>


### [32] [Deciding When to Rely on Visual Information: Gated Multimodal Fusion in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10700)
*Natalija Glisovic,Danica Kragic,Martin Tegner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VisGate框架，根据物品嵌入和用户序列上下文自适应地进行物品级别的视觉与协同信号融合决策，通过对比学习保留模态互补性，并利用门控机制作为工具来测量和理解视觉信息何时及为何对推荐有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态序列推荐系统将视觉与协同信号统一融合，将视觉特征视为通用的信息，忽略了视觉效用（visual utility）实际上是一个潜在的上下文变量，同时依赖于物品本身和用户的交互历史，而非固定的物品属性。

Method: VisGate框架包含两个关键设计：(1) 自适应物品级融合门控，基于物品嵌入和用户当前序列上下文决策是否以及如何融合视觉信号；(2) 通过序列共现模式的对比学习目标学习视觉表征，保持与协同嵌入的互补性而非对齐到共享空间。

Result: VisGate在推荐性能上具有竞争力，同时实验分析揭示了：视觉效用随物品而异，在交互稀疏、协同信号较弱时视觉效用增强，且与视觉独特性在语义上有意义地相关。

Conclusion: 研究强调了细粒度融合和模态互补性的重要性，证明物品级视觉效用可以通过学习到的门控行为进行估计和解释。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommender systems commonly fuse visual and collaborative signals uniformly, treating visual features as generically informative regardless of item or user context. We argue that visual utility, defined as the contribution of visual signals to recommendation quality, is a latent contextual variable that depends on both the item and the user's interaction history rather than a fixed item property. To model this variability, we introduce VisGate, a framework that makes adaptive item-level fusion decisions conditioned on item embeddings and the user's current sequence context. Visual representations are learned through a contrastive objective over sequential co-occurrence patterns, preserving complementarity with collaborative embeddings rather than aligning them into a shared space. Beyond achieving competitive recommendation performance, VisGate's learned gate serves as a measurement tool for understanding when and why visual information is beneficial. Our analyses show that visual utility varies across items, increases under interaction sparsity when collaborative signals are weak, and correlates with visual distinctiveness in semantically meaningful ways. Together, these findings highlight the importance of both fine-grained fusion and modality complementarity, while demonstrating that item-level visual utility can be estimated and interpreted through learned gating behaviour.

</details>


### [33] [TimeRoute: Time-Aware Modality Routing and Diffusion for Multi-Modal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10983)
*Pengyu Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Klim Zaporojets,Congfeng Cao,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeRoute，一个统一的多模态扩散推荐框架，通过时序感知的模态路由为每个用户分配个性化模态权重，并利用基于FiLM的条件扩散图重构器（含双流长短时去噪头）抑制过时模态边，解决模态时间尺度不匹配问题。在TikTok、Amazon-Baby、Amazon-Sports上相比强基线在Recall@K、Precision@K、NDCG@K上提升最高9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐器融合用户协作信号与文本、图像、音频等模态信息，但各模态的时效性随时间以不同速率漂移（如情人节期间巧克力购买从文本引导转向视觉包装和氛围音频）。这引发两个耦合挑战：(1) 不同时间上下文中用户需要不同的模态比例；(2) 相关性较弱的模态更可能引入过时或误导性信号。现有工作使用全局共享的融合权重，无法适应这种模态时间尺度不匹配。

Method: TimeRoute包含两大核心组件：一是时序感知的模态路由（temporal-aware modal router），将用户聚合行为特征映射为个性化模态分布，取代先前工作中的全局共享融合权重；二是基于扩散的图重构器，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）在同一时间画像上进行条件化，并配备双流（长期与短期）去噪头，在过时模态边进入传播图之前将其抑制。整体构建为统一扩散框架。

Result: 在TikTok、Amazon-Baby和Amazon-Sports三个数据集上，经过10次随机种子配对测试，TimeRoute在Recall@K、Precision@K和NDCG@K上均一致优于强基线，最高提升达9.8%。

Conclusion: TimeRoute通过将模态权重从全局静态变为用户与时间条件的个性化动态，并在扩散重建阶段主动过滤过时模态信号，有效解决了多模态推荐中模态时间尺度不匹配问题，验证了个性化时序模态感知对推荐性能的显著增益。

Abstract: Multi-modal recommenders fuse collaborative signals with item modalities such as text, images, and audio, but the usefulness of each drifts over time and at different rates. For example, chocolate purchases typically guided by textual ingredient cues can shift toward visual packaging and ambient audio around Valentine's Day. This modality time-scale mismatch gives rise to two coupled challenges: (1) users require different modality proportions across temporal contexts, and (2) less relevant modalities are more likely to introduce outdated or misleading signals into the recommender. We address both challenges within a unified diffusion-based recommender, TimeRoute. A temporal-aware modal router maps each user's aggregated behavioral features to a personalized modality distribution, replacing the globally shared fusion weights used in prior work. The diffusion-based graph reconstructor is then conditioned on the same temporal profile through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) with dual-stream long- and short-term denoising heads, suppressing outdated modality edges before they enter the propagation graph. Experiments on TikTok, Amazon-Baby, and Amazon-Sports demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 9.8\% in Recall@K, Precision@K, and NDCG@K over strong baselines across 10-seed paired tests. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TimeRoute.

</details>


### [34] [Sona Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11015)
*Sona Team,Alexandr Udeneev,Aleksei Krasilnikov,Alexey Nadtochiy,Andrey Semenov,Andrey Tsyrkunov,Anna Krivonos,Anna Lipkina,Artem Matveev,Daniil Burlakov,Daniil Leschev,Daria Tikhonovich,Denis Burshtein,Ekaterina Dmitrieva,Eugene Krofto,Grigorii Khlystov,Ilya Murzin,Kirill Golovko,Ksenia Sycheva,Leonid Dmitriev,Mariia Rozaeva,Mariia Ulianova,Mikhail Sandul,Nikolai Savushkin,Oleg Sorokin,Roman Odobesku,Semyon Panenko,Sergei Liamaev,Sergei Makeev,Vadim Shilov,Veronika Ivanova,Viktor Yanush,Vladimir Baikalov,Vladislav Dodonov,Vladislav Tytskiy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Sona是Yandex Music推出的单一生成式推荐模型，通过统一用户表示的编码器-解码器架构，同时完成候选生成和排序，在线上A/B测试中替代了由15+候选生成器和排序模型组成的生产级级联系统，显著提升了Active Users(+4.53%)、总收听时长(+6.30%)和点赞量(+11.42%)等关键指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统由多阶段级联组成：大量候选生成器+预排序+排序模型，消耗数百个特征，依赖大型Transformer模型（如Argus）和目标注意力打分器。这种多阶段架构复杂、部署成本高，且各阶段优化目标不一致。作者希望探索是否能用单一统一训练的模型替代成熟的级联系统，同时提升推荐质量。

Method: Sona采用编码器-解码器架构：编码器将用户按时间排序的交互事件序列转化为隐状态，供自回归解码器（负责候选生成）和排序模块（负责排序）共同使用。通过next-token-prediction和知识蒸馏两个目标联合训练编码器，使生成和排序通过同一用户状态耦合。训练阶段使用更大的教师模型提供排序目标，但推理阶段仅部署学生模型（编码器+解码器+排序模块）。模型不依赖任何手工特征，仅使用日志事件字段和学习得到的物品表示。

Result: 在Yandex Music最大推荐场景之一（My Vibe智能音箱流量）上进行的A/B实验中，Sona相对生产控制组取得统计显著的提升：Active Users（主要指标）+4.53%，总收听时长+6.30%，点赞量+11.42%。Active Users的提升是此前该场景最强模型Argus所带来增量幅度的2.35倍，且效果是增量的（叠加在先前部署改进之上）。

Conclusion: 实验证明，单一联合训练模型可以替代成熟的多阶段推荐级联系统，同时提升线上推荐质量。这说明"统一生成与排序"的架构范式具备实际落地价值，为推荐系统从复杂级联向单模型化演进提供了有力证据。

Abstract: We introduce Sona, a single-model generative recommender for Yandex Music. In an online A/B test, Sona replaced the entire production cascade, comprising more than 15 candidate generators followed by pre-ranking and ranking models that consume hundreds of features, including signals from large transformer models such as Argus and target-attention scorers, while significantly improving key engagement metrics.
  The architecture of Sona unifies candidate generation and ranking around a shared user representation. Its encoder transforms the user's chronological sequence of logged engagement events into hidden states consumed by both the autoregressive decoder and the Ranking Module. The next-token-prediction and distillation objectives jointly update the encoder, coupling generation and ranking through the same user state. Neither Sona nor its Teacher Ranker uses hand-engineered features; both operate on logged event fields and learned item representations. In the final Sona configuration, the larger teacher supplies ranking targets during training but is absent from serving, leaving the encoder, decoder, and Ranking Module as a single deployed model.
  We evaluate Sona in an online A/B experiment using live traffic from My Vibe on smart speakers, one of Yandex Music's largest recommendation surfaces. Relative to the production control, Sona produced statistically significant uplifts of 4.53% in Active Users, the primary metric, 6.30% in Total Listening Time, and 11.42% in Likes. These effects were incremental to improvements retained from preceding deployments. The Active Users uplift was 2.35 times the increment previously delivered by Argus, the strongest model deployed on this surface before Sona. These results show that a single jointly trained model can replace a mature multi-stage recommendation cascade while improving recommendation quality on live traffic.

</details>

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自知识RAG框架，引导大语言模型自动从专利匹配查询中提取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，结合FAISS检索与生成式匹配机制，显著提升了专利检索与匹配的准确率，避免了高额人工标注成本和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利文档结构复杂、技术术语密集且包含多模态信息，传统方法难以准确识别专利间的细微差异；现有基于LLM的专利匹配方法依赖领域特定的预训练或指令微调，存在高人工标注成本和灾难性遗忘问题；RAG方法引入外部知识但未能充分利用LLM自动解析专利和挖掘深层语义关系的能力。

Method: 提出一种自知识RAG（self-knowledge RAG）框架：引导LLM从专利匹配查询中自主提取关键技术实体并构建层级本体结构，实现查询扩展和精确检索；将FAISS向量检索与生成式匹配机制相结合，利用自知识增强模型对专利创新的理解能力。

Result: 在真实专利数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法在专利检索与匹配任务上表现优异，显著提升了检索与匹配的准确率，验证了该方法的有效性和应用潜力。

Conclusion: 自知识RAG框架通过让LLM自主解析专利并构建本体结构，有效解决了专利检索匹配中的细微差异识别难题，在无需大量人工标注的前提下实现了高精度检索与匹配，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Patent retrieval and matching based on large language models (LLMs) play a vital role in intellectual property protection. However, due to the complex structure of patent documents, dense technical terminology, and multi-modal information, traditional methods struggle to accurately identify subtle differences between patents. Existing LLM-based patent matching approaches typically rely on domain-specific pretrained or instruction tuning, which often entail high manual labeling costs and catastrophic forgetting. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods introduce external knowledge they fail to fully leverage LLM's capability to automatically parse patents and mine deep semantic relationships. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a self-knowledge RAG framework that guides LLMs to autonomously extract key technical entities and construct hierarchical ontological structures from patent matching queries, thereby enabling query expansion and precise retrieval. The method integrates the FAISS retrieval with a generative matching mechanism, leveraging self-knowledge to enhance the model's understanding of patent innovations and significantly improve retrieval and matching accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate the outstanding performance of the proposed method on real-world patent datasets, validating its effectiveness and application potential.

</details>


### [36] [Role of Personality in Conversational Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11164)
*Abdisalam Abukar,Junchen Fu,Chengli Zhai,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验考察了对话式信息搜寻中助手人格（外向型、尽责型、中性）与任务类型对用户行为及交互质量的联合影响，发现不存在全局最优人格，但用户强烈偏好人格风格的选择或自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于对话式信息搜寻，但助手在互动中不仅是检索或生成内容，还会塑造用户如何表达约束、提出追问、验证主张和判断答案是否充分。然而，用户人格、助手人格与任务情境如何共同影响这些交互，目前知之甚少。作者将人格视为可控变量，研究其对用户行为和交互质量的影响。

Method: 采用受控的受试者内设计：助手人格（外向型、尽责型、中性）和任务类型（探索性旅行规划、比较型智能手机购物、验证敏感性健康饮食信息搜寻）作为自变量进行实验操作；使用大五人格量表测量26名参与者的人格。数据收集包括对话日志、行为轨迹、交互后问卷、退出问卷和大五人格分数。

Result: 三种助手条件在行为上可区分：外向型助手产生更长的对话轮次；尽责型助手引发更高的用户词占比和更多轮次；中性基线介于两者之间。最强效应是任务与助手人格对信任和委托（delegation）的交互作用，偏好风格随任务而变化。

Conclusion: 不存在全局最优的助手人格设置，但参与者强烈偏好人格风格的选择或自适应。这一定位将助手人格视为一种情境敏感的交互设计变量，而非可全局优化的系统属性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for information seeking, where users find, compare, and evaluate information through dialogue. In this role, the assistant does more than retrieve or generate content: it shapes how users articulate constraints, ask follow-up questions, verify claims, and decide when an answer is sufficient for action. Yet little is known about how user personality, assistant personality, and task context jointly influence these interactions. We examine personality as a controllable variable in conversational information seeking and study its effects on user behaviour and interaction quality. We conducted a controlled within-subject study in which assistant personality and task type were experimentally varied, while participant personality was measured using Big Five scores. Twenty-six participants each completed three information-seeking tasks under three assistant personality conditions: extraverted, conscientious, and neutral. Tasks covered exploratory travel planning, comparative smartphone shopping, and verification-sensitive health and diet information seeking. Data included conversation logs, behavioural traces, post-interaction questionnaires, an exit questionnaire, and Big Five measures. The assistant conditions were behaviourally distinct: the extraverted assistant produced longer turns, the conscientious assistant elicited higher user word share and more turns, and the neutral baseline fell between them. The strongest effect was a task-by-assistant interaction on trust and delegation, with preferred styles varying by task. No global winner emerged, but participants strongly preferred style choice or adaptation. These findings position assistant personality as a context-sensitive interactional design variable rather than a globally optimisable system property.

</details>


### [37] [Are We Really Making Progress in Group Recommendation? Unmasking the Tie-Breaking Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11190)
*Song-Duo Ma,Pu-Jen Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 近期多个组推荐方法在标准基准上的性能提升可能源于评估偏差而非真实的偏好建模进步。该偏差源于训练时的分数压缩与评估时确定性平局打破之间的交互作用。作者提出平局感知评估协议，精确计算均匀随机平局打破下HR@K和NDCG@K的期望值，发现许多先前报告的性能提升在平局感知评估下大幅缩水，方法相对排名也发生显著变化。此外，额外的sigmoid变换在优化中可能起到隐式边际平滑作用，温度缩放的BPR可在避免严重平局膨胀的同时保留大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组推荐方法的性能提升可能并非反映真实建模能力的进步，而是由系统性评估偏差所致。该偏差源于训练阶段分数压缩与评估阶段确定性平局打破的交互，使top-K指标对平局处理方式高度敏感，从而产生虚假的性能改善。

Method: 作者提出平局感知评估协议，通过计算均匀随机平局打破下HR@K和NDCG@K的精确期望值来替代确定性平局打破。在CAMRa2011和Mafengwo数据集上，对近期代表性方法与基线在组推荐和用户推荐两种设置下进行重新评估，并进一步分析额外sigmoid变换在优化中的角色，提出温度缩放BPR作为替代方案。

Result: 实验表明，许多先前报告的改进在平局感知评估下显著缩小，方法的相对排名可能发生明显变化。额外sigmoid在优化中起到隐式边际平滑作用，而温度缩放BPR能够保留大部分该收益且不会引发严重的平局膨胀。

Conclusion: 平局感知评估对建立组推荐领域的可靠进展至关重要，研究结果强调了在评估中考虑平局处理方式的必要性。

Abstract: Recent group recommendation methods have reported strong improvements on standard benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains always reflect genuine advances in modeling group preferences. In this paper, we show that several recent methods are affected by a systematic evaluation bias caused by the interaction between training-time score compression and evaluation-time deterministic tie-breaking. Specifically, an additional sigmoid transformation before the BPR objective can greatly increase tied top scores, making top-K metrics such as HR@K and NDCG@K highly sensitive to how ties are resolved. We revisit recent representative methods and their baselines on CAMRa2011 and Mafengwo under both group and user recommendation settings, and evaluate them with a tie-aware protocol that computes the exact expectation of HR@K and NDCG@K under uniform random tie-breaking. Our results show that many previously reported improvements shrink substantially under tie-aware evaluation, and the relative ranking of methods can change markedly. We further show that the additional sigmoid may act as implicit margin smoothing during optimization, and that temperature-scaled BPR can retain much of this benefit without inducing severe tie inflation. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of tie-aware evaluation for establishing reliable progress in group recommendation. The code is available at https://github.com/songduoma/TieAwareGroupRec.

</details>

### [38] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将大语言模型（LLMs）从数据分析工具升级为自主AI引导的闭环实验框架，通过49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电）获取生物物理数据，LLM实时处理并自动触发硬件执行器以优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导可控胁迫场景。在垂直农场和单株植物三个案例中验证，生产级部署下最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%，能量优化模式降低能耗18%，并自主发现暗诱导叶绿素积累策略实现67.9%的节能，标志着LLM从人类参与分析向数字农业自主控制副驾驶的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统植物表型分析依赖人类专家介入，难以处理复杂生物系统的高维实时数据，且存在高计算成本和专家劳动力瓶颈。研究者希望通过LLM构建从数据采集、分析到硬件控制的闭环自主系统，实现AI与生物系统的直接交互，降低数字农业的成本-价值比。

Method: 基于49通道植物传感器网络（涵盖多光谱、电化学和介电模态）收集生物物理数据；LLM对数据进行实时处理与自然语言解读，评估植物生理状态；系统通过决策输出触发硬件执行器，实现微气候优化、表型协议执行和胁迫场景诱导；形成人类参与度递减直至完全自主的闭环架构（从human-in-the-loop到autonomous control）。生产级验证中Agent对全光谱、450nm和660nm光照以2小时间隔进行多参数优化。

Result: 在垂直农场与单株植物两个平台的三项案例研究中验证了框架有效性。生产级部署中：最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%；能量优化模式通过利用生理惯性进行光脉冲调控，能耗降低18%且栽培时间仅小幅增加；Agent自主发现暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%的节能效果。系统能解码植物生理中复杂的微宏观波动。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM从被动分析工具转变为数字农业的自主副驾驶和AI-生物学直接交互接口，实现了数据驱动的复杂生物系统与生态探索。闭环架构显著降低了计算资源与专家劳动力需求，改善了成本-价值比，展示了LLM在复杂生物学系统中从分析到自主控制的演进路径，为数字农业提供了可推广的范式。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [39] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT（Sampling Policy Observation Tree），一种模型无关、基于采样的深度强化学习策略可解释性框架。SPOT通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，构建一个有限时域的树结构，经验性地表征策略的动作偏好及其下游演化，并在SUMO-RL交通信号控制域中进行了案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习（DRL）智能体在复杂环境中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，缺乏一种通用的、模型无关的框架来直观揭示策略的动作偏好和后续演化路径。

Method: SPOT框架：给定策略和环境模拟器，通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态，构建一个可解释的有限时域树。树结构提供策略动作偏好的经验表示及其可能的下游演化。作者提供了形式化保证，证明SPOT能够渐近收敛到策略的唯一最可能动作，并刻画了其在高熵策略下的分歧行为。

Result: 在SUMO-RL交通信号控制案例中，SPOT的树状表示可用于检查策略偏好、比较备选未来轨迹，并揭示单步特征归因方法无法观察到的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT为DRL策略提供了一种可验证、可扩展的采样式可解释性方法，能够捕捉超越单步分析的长期行为模式，弥补了现有特征归因方法在揭示策略动态演化方面的不足。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [40] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDAS框架，通过变分建模将各模态表示为多元高斯潜变量，并分解为共享与专属因子，采用最小最大互信息目标实现稳定解缠与跨模态语义对齐，同时利用后验方差作为可靠性指标进行不确定性感知融合，有效处理多模态情感分析中的不完整模态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法假设输入模态完整，但真实应用中模态常不完整或损坏。已有方法依赖数据插补和启发式协调约束，无法有效从不完整多模态数据中提取和利用任务相关信息，亟需更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: MIDAS采用变分建模策略，用多元高斯潜变量表示每个模态并分解为共享因子和专属因子；设计最小最大目标函数最小化共享空间与专属空间之间的互信息以实现稳定解缠，同时最大化跨模态共享空间之间的互信息以增强语义对齐；引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指标自适应加权潜特征进行融合。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，MIDAS在多种不完整设置下相比竞争基线取得了稳定且一致性的性能提升，验证了其在不完整数据场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过互信息解缠与不确定性感知融合的统一框架，有效重构不完整条件下的多模态表征，为解决多模态情感分析中的模态不完整问题提供了可靠且有效的方案。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [41] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对绿色AI、绿色深度学习及减少AI模型环境影响的技术进行了系统综述，并对比了多种碳排放测量工具。作者还通过CPU实验环境对六个深度学习模型进行多标签分类任务的实证评估，发现训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源，且架构复杂度的增加并不总能带来成比例的精度提升，强调了在模型选择与AI系统设计中平衡预测性能与环境成本的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模深度学习模型的广泛部署，AI/ML的高能耗和碳排放问题日益受到关注。作者旨在系统梳理绿色AI领域的研究现状与优化技术，并通过实证评估揭示深度学习生命周期中各阶段的碳足迹分布，为可持续AI设计提供依据。

Method: 本文采用混合方法：(1) 对绿色AI、绿色深度学习及减排优化技术进行系统性文献综述；(2) 对比多种AI碳排放测量工具；(3) 在CPU实验环境中实现六个深度学习模型，执行多标签分类任务，以定量比较其整体碳排放并识别碳排放贡献最大的生命周期阶段。

Result: 实证结果显示训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源；同时发现模型架构复杂度的增加并不系统性转化为成比例的精度提升，提示存在性能与碳排放之间的失衡。

Conclusion: 研究强调应将对可持续性的考量整合进模型选择与AI系统设计流程中，在保持预测性能的同时降低环境影响，以实现更负责任的AI发展。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>

*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCBM，一种面向概念瓶颈模型的不确定性门控关系推理框架，通过在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥）并用不确定性引导关系精炼，从而在概念不可靠（缺失或翻转）的情况下实现稳健的推理，提升概念恢复和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: ReCBM框架包含三个关键组件：1）在瓶颈中引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），明确概念间证据交换方式；2）使用不确定性作为门控信号，引导概念关系的精炼过程；3）通过不确定性调制每个概念在关系推理中的贡献度，实现对不可靠概念状态的鲁棒处理。

Result: 在多数据集上的实验表明，ReCBM在概念缺失和翻转场景下提升了概念恢复与任务恢复性能；支持不确定性感知的测试时干预；能提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集且不降低下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ReCBM通过不确定性门控的关系推理有效解决了CBMs在不可靠概念状态下的稳健推理问题，为概念瓶颈模型提供了一种更鲁棒、更可解释的推理范式。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>

Conclusion: 自动化行为科学研究能够对AI智能体的人工研究进行补充并扩展其研究覆盖面，证明了自动化方法在该领域的可行性及价值。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>

*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个面向硬件验证测试平台激励生成的后训练框架，通过阶段式有监督微调产生行为多样化的检查点，再利用密集奖励强化学习将其转化为各具优势的专家模型，最终通过免训练的模型合并或进一步后训练整合这些互补能力，将4B模型在CVDP-ECov上达到88.0% Pass@1，超越DeepSeek-R1（671B）13.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件验证是现代芯片设计中工作量占比可观的重要任务，高覆盖率测试平台激励生成是关键环节。传统仅依赖文本模仿的微调方法缺乏可靠的学习信号，而可执行的反馈能够提供更优于文本模仿的学习依据。作者希望超越传统SFT到RL的标准后训练流程，进一步提升LLM在硬件验证代码生成上的性能。

Method: CHORUS框架包含两个关键观察并据此设计：(1) 采用阶段式SFT生成行为多样化的模型检查点，随后通过密集奖励的RL将这些检查点训练为综合表现相当但任务层面优势各异的专家模型；(2) 通过免训练的模型合并（model merging）或进一步的后训练，将专家的互补能力整合为单一4B模型，从而超越任何单个专家的表现。

Result: CHORUS在CVDP-ECov基准上达到88.0%的Pass@1，以4B参数规模超越了DeepSeek-R1（671B参数）13.5个百分点，证明了通过专家整合得到的紧凑模型能够大幅超越大规模模型。

Conclusion: 阶段式SFT与密集奖励RL结合能够产生能力互补的专家模型，而通过模型合并或进一步后训练将这些专家整合，可以显著提升最终模型的性能，使小规模模型在特定任务上超越超大模型的体量优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [45] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MESA框架，针对长时程智能体轨迹中多结构记忆的查询自适应动态选择与融合问题，通过端到端答案级弱监督学习，在AMA-Bench上超越最强基线8.5%并减少41%的证词token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的轨迹包含数百步推理、行动和观察步骤，查询答案可能依赖历史深处被埋没的证据。外部记忆以多种结构化表示存储轨迹，但每种结构只提供不完整视角。现有多记忆系统要么对所有查询固定读取全部结构导致上下文膨胀和噪声引入，要么将查询路由到单一结构而无法组合互补证据。受控分析证明最优记忆配置既非单一结构也非全量合并，而是随查询和任务变化的结构组合。

Method: MESA包含五个互补的结构视角来建模每条轨迹，并通过端到端答案级反馈学习为冻结的答案模型选择和融合查询自适应子集。为了在弱监督下学习，MESA采用带先验引导搜索的harness优化和UCB引导调度来平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在AMA-Bench上，MESA相比最强基线提升8.5%的性能，同时相比全结构方案减少41%的证词token使用。

Conclusion: 结构级动态选择是长时程智能体多记忆管理的有效范式，查询自适应的结构组合能在提升性能的同时大幅降低上下文开销。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [46] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，认为智能体AI治理实为四个不同问题而非一个问题：个体智能体（控制理论）、智能体群体（复杂适应系统理论）、人机团队（监督控制论）和智能体舰队（工程运营）。论文通过三项实证研究揭示了"涌现鸿沟"——风险在涌现层实现而能力几乎缺失、实践完全空白，并提出五级成熟度模型将其落地为科学成熟度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI智能体的速度远超其治理能力，现行做法错误地用单一学科（如DevSecOps，本为确定性自动化设计）覆盖所有尺度的自主性，导致治理体系结构性失效。

Method: 提出CASE框架，将智能体治理拆分为四个层面：将控制理论应用于个体智能体（意图为设定点、护栏为反馈、评估为观测）；将复杂适应系统理论应用于智能体群体（涌现性使单智能体保证不具备组合性）；将监督控制论应用于人机团队（必要多样性法则证明无辅助的人工监督在结构上必然失败）；将工程运营应用于舰队（将错误预算扩展至决策质量）。形式化各层并推导跨层耦合条件，包括"零接触部署悖论"。

Result: 三项实证研究：82%已记录的生产环境智能体故障是多层轨迹；22个生态工具中无一对第2层（涌现）提供完整覆盖；全部35个评分的公开部署均处于最低成熟度区间。由此定义"涌现鸿沟"。提出五级成熟度模型，采用非补偿性瓶颈加权指数和评估工具。

Conclusion: 鉴于EU AI Act第14条将有效人工监督设为法律要求，只有满足必要多样性法则的架构才能使监督成为现实而非仪式性的存在。CASE为智能体治理提供了基于成熟科学而非流程成熟度的范式基础。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [47] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SBCO（自监督块坐标优化器），一种基于验证器引导的闭循环优化方法，用于带显式约束的规划任务。与自指式的Gödel机器（如Darwin和Huxley Gödel Machine）不同，SBCO采用自监督而非自指方式，通过分解的验证器银行和近似块坐标上升的harness策略学习来改进智能体输出，无需人类标签和固定元智能体，在计算开销上比自修改基线节省4-5.5倍，同时性能匹敌或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自指式自我改进方法（如Darwin/Huxley Gödel Machine）要求任务胜任力与自修改胜任力高度对齐（仅适用于编码任务），而在不对齐的领域只能通过移除自指性或引入元智能体显式自修改来实现，这两种方式都计算昂贵且依赖种群或搜索策略。对于带显式约束的规划任务，需要一种更廉价的自改进替代方案。

Method: SBCO通过与Gödel机器家族相同的闭循环、从经验中改进的范式，但采用自监督而非自指方式。具体而言，给定一个智能体harness，SBCO学习一个分解的验证器银行（decomposed bank of verifiers）和一个harness策略，通过近似块坐标上升（approximate block coordinate ascent）从自身分级反馈中改进智能体的输出，使用固定元智能体且无需人类标注。

Result: 在两个领域的实验中，SBCO匹配或超越了定制化的自修改基线方法，同时计算预算减少了4-5.5倍。

Conclusion: SBCO为带显式约束的规划任务提供了一种高效、廉价的自我改进方案，在保持与更昂贵的自指式方法相当性能的同时，大幅降低了计算开销，拓展了自我改进方法在不满足胜任力对齐条件的任务中的适用范围。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [48] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于GFlowNets的自动化、无需人工干预的自适应红队方法，利用一个大语言模型测试另一个大语言模型，以发现LLM的安全漏洞并给出量化鲁棒性评分，同时首次引入能够生成土耳其语攻击输入的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在各领域的广泛集成，安全漏洞日益突出。现有红队测试要么依赖专家人工测试（耗时），要么依赖预定义攻击数据集（缺乏创造力和适应性），亟需一种自动化、自适应且无需人工参与的红队评估方法。

Method: 利用GFlowNets框架，训练一个攻击者模型（attacker）针对特定的受害者模型（victim）执行自动化红队测试，实现一个大语言模型检测另一个大语言模型漏洞的目标，并为受害者模型提供量化鲁棒性评分。

Result: 该方法在英语环境中能够生成比现有基准更有效的对抗性攻击；此外，作为文献中的新贡献，该方法还支持生成土耳其语攻击输入。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种适应性强、自动化程度高的红队测试方案，不仅提升了英语对抗攻击的有效性，还扩展到了土耳其语场景，为LLM安全评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [49] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种基于检索的、约束感知的对话引擎，用于公共服务的可靠推荐。该框架通过双数据表示模式将用户查询解析为结构和语义约束，从而提升检索质量，减少大语言模型的幻觉推荐，并提高用户约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务聊天机器人需要在嘈杂且不一致的服务目录上提供满足用户明确约束的可靠推荐，而通用大语言模型经常生成不可靠的内容并引用未经验证的网络来源。现有方法忽略了检索质量对约束感知推荐的影响，因此需要系统研究检索质量在此类系统中的关键作用。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，包含：(1) 将用户查询解析为结构和语义约束的解析模块；(2) 基于双数据表示模式（含知识图谱变体）的检索模块；(3) 使用州级食品储藏室目录和合成查询基准进行评测，比较多种知识表示变体（有无知识图谱）以及多个开源LLM和一个专有模型。

Result: 增强检索能大幅提高用户约束满足度并减少幻觉推荐；随着检索质量的提升，不同LLM之间的性能差距缩小，结果对模型大小不再敏感。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健公共服务对话系统的关键因素，优于单纯依赖更大模型规模的方案。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [50] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对Vision Wormhole（潜空间视觉通信系统）中的密集张量传输进行冗余性分析。通过后验稀疏自编码器（SAE）对冻结的激活进行压缩，实现了128倍的传输字节缩减（从原始float32传输到uint16索引/float16值的稀疏载荷，k=4），同时将七任务非AIME平均准确率从49.85%微降至49.77%，并发现4096元素字典仅使用50个有效特征，任务间激活集的平均对Jaccard相似度为0.906。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构视觉-语言模型智能体通过潜空间通信传输连续表示时，Vision Wormhole将视觉特征翻译为通用潜表示，但每次消息都以相同大小的密集张量传输，无论内容如何。固定容量的密集张量不一定有固定的有效信息密度——某些消息可能仅使用可用表示自由度的一小部分，这意味着通信信道可能存在显著的可压缩性。

Method: 对冻结的Vision Wormhole激活进行后验稀疏自编码器（SAE）拟合，测量重建质量、下游效用、特征复用和token级干预；在九个推理基准上评估uint16索引/float16值的稀疏载荷（k=4）相对于原始float32密集传输的效果。

Result: 稀疏载荷将传输字节数减少128倍；七任务非AIME平均准确率从49.85%微降至49.77%（单次运行）。4096元素的字典仅使用了50个特征，任务级激活集的平均对Jaccard相似度为0.906，表明特征高度跨任务复用且通信高度可压缩。

Conclusion: 强后验可压缩性已被证实，但尚不能隔离稀疏编码相对于位置选择、精度降低、低秩结构或SAE优化效应的增量贡献。需要后续进行匹配载荷（matched-payload）的比较实验，并探索随每条消息实际信息需求自适应的通信机制。

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>

Method: 作者在音素检测竞赛中发现，采用多任务学习策略——让一个轻量级模型同时预测学习者的年龄和音素序列——可以显著提升模型在目标分布上的性能。该模型仅94M参数，规模远小于WavLM Large（317M）。

Result: 94M参数的轻量级模型在DrivenData目标分布上超越了317M参数的WavLM Large模型，并且与参数量为前者90倍的竞赛集成模型相比，CER仅差约0.04。

Conclusion: 该研究成果催生了PhonemeTrainer应用，可在大多数现代手机上运行，将儿童语音处理推向边缘端，从而在改进儿童语音ASR和发音辅助应用的同时，获得隐私保护和合规性方面的优势。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech.
